# The First World War: Shot List and Image Prompts (Part 2 of 3)

Each line of the voiceover is one shot of about 1–3 seconds. Each prompt box is **self-contained**: paste it on its own into any image model (Midjourney, GPT-Image, Flux, Ideogram, etc.) and it carries the full style, characters and scene.

**Style target:** the reference frames, i.e. a flat hand-drawn 2D explainer cartoon with big round heads, stick limbs, thick wobbly black outlines, one cel-shade tone, a muted palette and handwritten captions.

**Tips:** use 16:9 (e.g. `--ar 16:9`). To keep characters consistent, generate a character sheet first and pass it as a reference image together with these prompts. Durations assume about 2.7 spoken words per second.

**Total shots:** 429 · **Estimated runtime:** ~15 min 58 s

**This part:** The July Crisis → New Weapons of War · 141 shots

## Character sheet (reference)

- ARTHUR (recurring young British man, 19): round beige head, short ginger-brown hair neatly parted, a few tiny freckle dots, kind eyes. Civilian: white collarless shirt, brown waistcoat, grey flat cap. As a soldier: khaki British service tunic and trousers, khaki puttees wrapped around the shins, brown leather webbing, rifle; khaki peaked cap in 1914, flat round 'Brodie' steel helmet from 1916.
- ARTHUR'S MOTHER (50s): round beige head, brown hair streaked with grey in a neat bun, long dark-green Edwardian dress with a high collar, cream shawl.
- FRENCH SOLDIER (poilu): round beige head, big bushy black mustache. 1914: dark-blue greatcoat, bright red trousers, red-and-blue kepi cap. From 1915: horizon-blue greatcoat and the crested blue-grey Adrian steel helmet.
- GERMAN SOLDIER: round beige head, blond mustache. Field-grey uniform and greatcoat, black leather boots; 1914: spiked leather Pickelhaube helmet with grey cloth cover; from 1916: coal-scuttle-shaped grey Stahlhelm steel helmet.
- RUSSIAN SOLDIER: round beige head, brown beard, khaki-olive gymnastyorka tunic belted at the waist, soft peaked khaki cap, rolled greatcoat over one shoulder.
- ARCHDUKE FRANZ FERDINAND (heir to Austria-Hungary, simplified caricature in this style): round beige head, short dark hair, large upturned dark mustache, pale-blue Austrian general's tunic with gold collar, red sash, tall dark cap topped with a tuft of green feathers.
- SOPHIE, DUCHESS OF HOHENBERG (Franz Ferdinand's wife, simplified caricature): round beige head, dark hair up, long white Edwardian dress, wide white hat with a white ostrich feather, small bouquet of red roses.
- GAVRILO PRINCIP (19-year-old Bosnian Serb, simplified caricature): thin small body, round pale-beige head, messy dark hair, faint dark mustache shadow, sunken intense eyes, plain dark suit and white shirt.
- KAISER WILHELM II (German Emperor, simplified caricature): round beige head, huge sharply upturned waxed mustache, grey military greatcoat with medals, silver-topped spiked Pickelhaube helmet.
- TSAR NICHOLAS II (Russian Emperor, simplified caricature): round beige head, neat short brown beard and mustache, olive-khaki military tunic with a few medals, khaki peaked cap.
- VLADIMIR LENIN (simplified caricature): round beige head, bald domed top with reddish-brown hair at the sides, short reddish-brown goatee and mustache, dark three-piece suit, flat cap held in one hand.
- MANFRED VON RICHTHOFEN, 'THE RED BARON' (simplified caricature): young, round beige head, light-brown slicked hair, brown leather flying helmet with goggles pushed up, long brown leather flying coat, plain white scarf.
- WOODROW WILSON (28th US President, simplified caricature): round beige head, grey hair, rimless pince-nez glasses, long serious face shape within the round head, dark suit.


---

## The July Crisis

### Shot 138 · ~1s

> **VO:** THE JULY CRISIS

```text
FORMAT: 16:9 widescreen landscape image (1920x1080), aspect ratio 16:9.

STYLE: 2D flat vector cartoon frame in a hand-drawn educational YouTube explainer style, 16:9 widescreen (1920x1080), artwork fills the entire frame edge to edge with NO black bars or borders. Thick, even, slightly wobbly hand-inked black outlines (about 5px) on every shape. Characters are simple 'bean people': very large perfectly round heads (almost as wide as the torso), small rounded gumdrop/bean-shaped torsos, arms and legs drawn as single thin black lines, tiny simple mitten hands, no detailed feet. Faces have no nose, small solid black oval dot eyes, short curved eyebrow strokes that carry all the emotion, and a tiny simple mouth. Skin is warm beige (#E5CDB2) with ONE flat, hard-edged, darker beige cel-shade crescent on one side of the head. Hair is a solid flat shape with one darker shade. Flat colours only, one hard-edged shadow tone per object, no gradients, no texture, no glossy highlights. Muted, desaturated palette: dusty blue-greys, warm beige, taupe, olive green, soft browns, slate, with only small saturated accents (red, mustard yellow). Backgrounds are simple and flat with minimal detail, clean architectural lines, simple one- or two-point perspective, plain flat-coloured walls; soft flat grey ellipse shadows under standing characters. Any on-screen text is a casual handwritten marker/script font (black letters, often with a thick white outline).

SETTING ERA: Europe and the wider world, 1900-1919 unless the scene says otherwise; accurate Edwardian / First World War period clothing, uniforms, vehicles, weapons and architecture, all simplified into the flat cartoon style.

HISTORICAL CONTEXT: July-August 1914: diplomats in ornate government offices, telegrams, declarations of war, mobilising armies and crowded railway stations.

SCENE: Chapter title card. Flat stormy grey background filling the frame, a simple hand-drawn calendar page for July 1914 illustrated small and centred under the title, generous empty space, gentle subtle vignette in a slightly darker flat shade at the corners.

ON-SCREEN TEXT: "The July Crisis" in the handwritten marker font, clearly legible, spelled exactly.

MOOD: Clean, calm, inviting title card.

AVOID: photorealism, 3D render, anime/manga, chibi sparkle eyes, gradients, airbrush, glossy shading, noses, realistic hands or fingers, thin sketchy pencil lines, watercolour or paper texture, extra or misspelled text, watermarks, signatures, real brand logos.

ASPECT RATIO: 16:9 (widescreen landscape) --ar 16:9
```
